            start = time.monotonic()
            with self.assertRaises(modbus.ModbusTimeout):
                client.read_registers(modbus.READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
            self.assertTrue(client.closed)
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - start, 0.8)
        finally:
            client.close()
            server.close()

    def test_p05_deadline_restarts_for_each_request(self):
        server = ReplyServer([0.015, 0.015])
        client = modbus.ModbusClient(server.port, timeout=0.5)
        try:
            self.assertEqual(client.read_registers(modbus.READ_HOLDING, 0, 1), [7])
            time.sleep(0.55)
            self.assertEqual(client.read_registers(modbus.READ_HOLDING, 0, 1), [7])
        finally:
            client.close()
            server.close()

    def test_p06_positive_subprecision_guard_still_rounds_inward(self):
        multiplier = "0." + "0" * 34 + "1"
        actual = msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", multiplier, 0.1)
        self.assertEqual(actual, (Decimal("7.001"), Decimal("12.999")), repr(actual))

    def test_p07_large_valid_guard_is_unavailable_not_crash(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            result = cli("src.msa", small_config(folder, guard_multipliers=["1" + "0" * 40]), cwd=folder)
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
            with (Path(folder) / "reports/msa_guard.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
                rows = list(csv.DictReader(source))
            self.assertEqual(next(row for row in rows if row["section"] == "development")["status"], msa.NO_GUARD)
            self.assertEqual(next(row for row in rows if row["section"] == "holdout")["value"], msa.NOT_VERIFIED)

    def test_p08_structural_rejections_create_no_reports(self):
        for changes in ({"gauges": {"good": None}}, {"gauges": {"good": []}},
                        {"gauges": {"good\n": RAW["gauges"]["good"]}},
                        {"guard_multipliers": ["0", "00.000"]}, {"holdout_seeds": "0-0"},
                        {"max_false_accept": True}, {"inspection": " "}):
            with self.subTest(changes=changes), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
                result = cli("src.msa", small_config(folder, **changes), cwd=folder)
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 3, result.stderr)
                self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports").exists())

    def test_p10_fraction_anova_shift_and_scale_invariance(self):
625:def show(value):

    def service(route, index):
        station = stations[index]
        time = station.times.get(route.model, 0)
        if station.inspection is None:
            return time
        return route.attempts.get(station.inspection, 0) * time

    def target_after(route, index):
        if index >= 0 and stations[index].inspection and stations[index].inspection == route.end_inspection:
            return None
        for position in range(index + 1, len(stations)):
            if service(route, position) > 0:
                return position
        return None

    def begin(route, index):
        nonlocal order
        for number, server in enumerate(servers[index]):
            if server[0] == "idle":
                duration = service(route, index)
                server[:] = ["busy", route, clock]
                order += 1
                heapq.heappush(events, (clock + duration, order, index, number))
                result.work[stations[index].id] += duration
                return True
        return False

def show(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""
    number = Decimal(value.numerator) / Decimal(value.denominator)
    return str(number.quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP))


def _blank(columns, section, **fields):
    row = {column: "" for column in columns}
    row["section"] = section
    row.update(fields)
    return row


def metric_row(section, method, metrics, threshold="", candidates=""):
    return _blank(METHOD_COLUMNS, section, method=method, threshold=threshold, candidates=candidates,
                  detection_rate=show(metrics.detection_rate), early=metrics.early, missed=metrics.missed,
                  mean_delay_cost=show(metrics.mean_cost), mean_detected_delay=show(metrics.mean_detected),
                  false_per_1000=show(metrics.false_per_1000))


def shift_rows(section, method, metrics):
    return [_blank(METHOD_COLUMNS, section, method=method, shift=shift, detection_rate=show(metrics.shift_rate(shift)))
            for shift in sorted(metrics.by_shift)]


def policy_row(method, metrics, threshold, settings):
    minutes = metrics.stop_minutes_per_1000 * Fraction(str(settings.repair_minutes))
    return _blank(METHOD_COLUMNS, "policy", method=method, threshold=threshold,
                  suspect_output=show(metrics.suspect_output), false_stops=show(metrics.false_per_1000),
                  stop_minutes=show(minutes))

class RefusalTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def refused(self, change):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            config = prepare(folder)
            change(Path(folder))
            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
            self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports" / "spc_methods.csv").exists())

    def test_t217_missing_row(self):
        def drop(folder):
            edit(folder / "reports" / "anomaly_methods.csv",
                 lambda text: "\n".join(line for line in text.split("\n") if not line.startswith("holdout,logistic")))
        self.refused(drop)

    def test_t254_setting_changed(self):
        self.refused(lambda folder: edit(folder / "reports" / "anomaly_model.csv",
                                         lambda text: text.replace("setting,window,10", "setting,window,11")))

    def test_t254_split_changed(self):
        self.refused(lambda folder: edit(folder / "reports" / "anomaly_model.csv",
                                         lambda text: text.replace("split,holdout,200-211", "split,holdout,200-212")))

    def test_t254_rule_row_changed(self):
        def change(folder):
            path = folder / "reports" / "anomaly_methods.csv"
            rows = path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8-sig").split("\r\n")
            index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(rows) if line.startswith("validation,rule_3_2,,"))
            cells = rows[index].split(",")
            cells[8] = "999.0000"
            rows[index] = ",".join(cells)
            path.write_bytes("\r\n".join(rows).encode("utf-8-sig"))
        self.refused(change)

    def test_t254_length_only_changed(self):
        def change(folder):
            write_anomaly(folder, length=119)
        self.refused(change)

    def test_t253_short_scenarios_exit_2(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            config = prepare(folder, length=4, onset_min=2, onset_max=3, window=2)
            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
            self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports" / "spc_methods.csv").exists())

    def test_t253_zero_range_exit_2(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            config = prepare(folder)
            current = os.getcwd()
            os.chdir(folder)
            try:
                with mock.patch("src.spc.normalized", lambda values, process: [0.0] * len(values)):
                    with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as caught, mock.patch("sys.stderr"):
                        spc.main([str(config)])
            finally:
                os.chdir(current)
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, 2)
            self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports" / "spc_methods.csv").exists())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

def show(value):
    """분수를 소수 넷째 자리 ROUND_HALF_UP으로 표시한다. 비교에는 쓰지 않는다.

    입력 자릿수에 상한이 없으므로 Decimal 문맥을 거치지 않고 정수로 반올림한다.
    """
    fraction = Fraction(value)
    scaled = abs(fraction) * 10000
    whole, part = divmod((2 * scaled.numerator + scaled.denominator) // (2 * scaled.denominator), 10000)
    sign = "-" if fraction < 0 and (whole or part) else ""
    return "%s%d.%04d" % (sign, whole, part)


def uph(count, end):
    return Fraction(count * 3_600_000, end) if end else Fraction(0)


def cycle_limits(takt, count, visits, good_rate):
    """명목 상한과 양품 보정 상한이다. 방문이 없으면 None이다."""
    if not visits:
        return None
    return takt * count / visits, takt * count * good_rate / visits

            def bodies(text):
                tree = ast.parse(text)
                return {node.name: ast.dump(node, include_attributes=False) for node in ast.walk(tree)
                        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)) and node.name.startswith("test_")}
            before, after = bodies(old), bodies(current)
            for key, value in before.items():
                self.assertEqual(after.get(key), value, name + ":" + key)
                count += 1
        artifacts = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "reports", "recipes", "data"], cwd=ROOT).decode().splitlines()
        for name in artifacts:

            current = subprocess.check_output(["git", "hash-object", name], cwd=ROOT, stderr=subprocess.PIPE).strip()
            self.assertEqual(current, old, name)

class MsaError(ValueError):
    """설정 문제를 위치와 함께 모아서 보고한다. 종료 코드는 3이다."""

    def __init__(self, errors):
        super().__init__("\n".join(errors))
        self.errors = errors


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Gauge:
    repeatability: float
    operator: float
    interaction: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    generator: Path
    inspection: str
    parts: int
    operators: int
    trials: int
    study_seed: int
    gauges: dict
    guard_multipliers: tuple
    products: int
    dev_seeds: range
    holdout_seeds: range
    max_false_accept: Fraction


def parse_seeds(text):
    """`시작-끝`을 range로 읽는다. 잘못된 문자열은 None이다."""
    match = SEEDS.fullmatch(text) if isinstance(text, str) else None
    if match is None:
        return None
    start, end = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
    return range(start, end + 1) if start <= end <= MAX_SEED else None


def _check_gauges(gauges, errors):
    if not isinstance(gauges, dict) or not 1 <= len(gauges) <= 10:
        errors.append("$.gauges: 1–10개의 객체여야 함")
        return
    for name, gauge in gauges.items():
        location = "$.gauges." + name
        if not GAUGE_NAME.fullmatch(name):
            errors.append(location + ": 이름 규칙 위반")
        if not _keys(gauge, GAUGE_KEYS, location, errors) or set(gauge) != set(GAUGE_KEYS):
            continue
        for key in GAUGE_KEYS:
            if not _number(gauge[key]) or not 0 <= gauge[key] <= MAX_RATIO:
                errors.append(location + "." + key + ": 0–100의 유한한 수여야 함")
        if _number(gauge["repeatability"]) and gauge["repeatability"] == 0:
            errors.append(location + ".repeatability: 0 초과여야 함")


def _check_multipliers(values, errors):
    location = "$.guard_multipliers"
    if not isinstance(values, list) or not values:
        errors.append(location + ": 비어 있지 않은 목록이어야 함")
        return
    if not all(isinstance(value, str) and MULTIPLIER.fullmatch(value) for value in values):
        errors.append(location + ": 소수 문자열이어야 함")
        return
    numbers = [Decimal(value) for value in values]
    if any(left >= right for left, right in zip(numbers, numbers[1:])):
        errors.append(location + ": 중복 없이 오름차순이어야 함")

+"""Review 021: focused probes; all generated outputs use tempfile directories."""

+from fractions import Fraction

+from src import anomaly, msa, spc

+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "5d46efe9975be42e18fb5b99ceda726e55984c88"
+UNIT = spc.Limits(0.0, 2.326, 1.0, 3.0, -3.0, 10.0, 1.0, 2)
+TUNING = spc.Tuning(0.2, 3, 0.5, 1.5)

+def raw(name):
+    return json.loads((ROOT / "recipes" / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+
+
+def dump(path, data):
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def cli(module, *args, cwd):
+    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT), PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8", PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=cwd, env=env,
+                          capture_output=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=240)
+
+
+def rows(path):
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def write_rows(path, records):
+    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=tuple(records[0]))
+        writer.writeheader()
+        writer.writerows(records)
+
+
+def generator(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("generator_measured.json")
+    data.update(recipe=str(ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_measured.json"),
+                equipment=str(ROOT / "recipes/equipment_measured.json"))
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "generator.json", data)
+
+
+def msa_config(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("msa_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), parts=2, operators=2, trials=2,
+                products=30, dev_seeds="0-0", holdout_seeds="100-100", gauges={"good": data["gauges"]["good"]})
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "msa.json", data)
+
+
+def prepare(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("anomaly_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), length=40, window=4,
+                onset_min=12, onset_max=25, iterations=2,
+                splits={name: {"seed_start": start, "count": 12}
+                        for name, start in zip(anomaly.SPLITS, (0, 100, 200))})
+    data.update(changes)
+    anomaly_path = dump(Path(folder) / "anomaly.json", data)
+    result = cli("src.anomaly", anomaly_path, cwd=folder)
+    if result.returncode:
+        raise AssertionError(result.stderr)
+    config = raw("spc_lamp.json")
+    config.update(anomaly="anomaly.json", anomaly_report="reports/anomaly_methods.csv",
+                  anomaly_model="reports/anomaly_model.csv")
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "spc.json", config)

+class Probe021(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_p01_exact_guards_at_negative_bounds_and_tiny_width(self):
+        tiny = "0." + "0" * 34 + "1"
+        for lower, upper, expected in (("7", "13", ("7.001", "12.999")),
+                                       ("-13", "-7", ("-12.999", "-7.001")),
+                                       ("-3", "3", ("-2.999", "2.999"))):
+            self.assertEqual(tuple(map(str, msa.acceptance_limits(lower, upper, tiny, 0.1))), expected)
+        self.assertIsNone(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "1" + "0" * 40, 0.1))
+        self.assertEqual(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "0", 0.1), (Decimal("7"), Decimal("13")))
+        print("P01 tiny/negative/huge/zero guards: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p02_fixed_msa_extremes_complete_cli(self):
+        for case in ("large_guard", "large_cpk"):
+            with self.subTest(case=case), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                changes = ({"guard_multipliers": ["1" + "0" * 40]} if case == "large_guard"
+                           else {"generator": str(generator(folder, process_cpk=1e200))})
+                result = cli("src.msa", msa_config(folder, **changes), cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                reports = Path(folder) / "reports"
+                self.assertEqual(len(list(reports.glob("msa_*.csv"))), 3)
+                if case == "large_guard":
+                    dev = next(row for row in rows(reports / "msa_guard.csv") if row["section"] == "development")
+                    self.assertEqual(dev["status"], msa.NO_GUARD)
+                else:
+                    self.assertEqual(Decimal(rows(reports / "msa_grr.csv")[0]["cpk_obs"]), Decimal("1e200"))
+                print("P02 %s: exit=0, reports=3" % case, flush=True)
+
+    def test_p03_observed_cpk_across_scales_and_display_ties(self):
+        for sigma in (1e-200, 1.0, 1e100):
+            cpk = 1 / sigma
+            for ratio in (0, 0.5, 2):
+                value = msa.observed_cpk(cpk, sigma, ratio * sigma)
+                self.assertAlmostEqual(value / cpk, 1 / math.hypot(1, ratio))
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(1.23445), "1.2345")
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(-1.23445), "-1.2345")
+        print("P03 scale and signed HALF_UP: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p04_tiny_valid_ewma_lambda_keeps_positive_limit(self):
+        tuning = spc.Tuning(1e-20, 3, 0.5, 1.5)
+        chart = spc.Ewma(UNIT, tuning)
+        actual = spc.first_alarm(chart, [0.1] * 12, 9)
+        stable_limit = 3 * math.sqrt(tuning.ewma_lambda / (2 - tuning.ewma_lambda)
+                                     * -math.expm1(24 * math.log1p(-tuning.ewma_lambda)))
+        print("P04 alarm=%r limit=%r expected_no_alarm stable_limit=%r" %
+              (actual, chart.limit, stable_limit), flush=True)
+        self.assertIsNone(actual, "Valid lambda collapses the EWMA limit to zero")
+
+    def test_p05_prewarm_and_joint_rules_at_offgrid_start(self):
+        limits = spc.Limits(0, 2.326, 1, 10, -10, 1, 1, 8)
+        values = [2.5] * 10 + [0.5, 1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 4.5]
+        alarm, active = spc.subgroup_alarm(values, limits, 10, True)
+        self.assertEqual((alarm, active), (14, ["range", "run_2of3"]))
+        counts = spc.count_triggers([active])
+        self.assertEqual((counts["range"], counts["run_2of3"], counts["joint"]), (1, 0, 1))
+        self.assertEqual(spc.first_alarm(spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING), [0, 0, -1, -2], 0), 3)
+        chart = spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING)
+        for value in (0, 0, -1, -2):
+            chart.update(value)
+        self.assertEqual(chart.change_point(), 2)
+        print("P05 prewarm, range/run priority and lower CUSUM: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p06_decision_denominator_exhaustive_offgrid_intervals(self):
+        for first in range(2, 15):
+            for last in range(0, 31):
+                expected = sum(t % 5 == 4 for t in range(first, last + 1))
+                self.assertEqual(spc.decision_count("xbar_we", first, last), expected)
+        outcomes = [(0, None, 14), (1, 13, None), (1, 18, 14)]
+        self.assertEqual(spc.false_per_1000_decisions("xbar_r", outcomes, 40, 10), Fraction(2000, 2))
+        print("P06 403 offgrid intervals and pre-onset exposure: correct", flush=True)

+    def test_p08_rule_metrics_collision_does_not_prove_same_measurements(self):

+    def test_p10_original_tests_and_artifacts_preserved(self):
+        def git(*args):
+            return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
+
+        def bodies(text):
+            found = {}
+            def visit(node, prefix=()):
+                for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
+                    if isinstance(child, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
+                        key = prefix + (child.name,)
+                        if child.name.startswith("test_"):
+                            found[key] = ast.dump(child, include_attributes=False)
+                        visit(child, key)
+                    else:
+                        visit(child, prefix)
+            visit(ast.parse(text))
+            return found
+
+        count = 0
+        for name in git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "tests").decode().splitlines():
+            if name.endswith(".py"):
+                before = bodies(git("show", BASE + ":" + name).decode("utf-8-sig"))
+                after = bodies((ROOT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+                for key, value in before.items():
+                    self.assertEqual(after.get(key), value, name + ":" + ".".join(key))
+                    count += 1
+        artifacts = git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "reports", "recipes", "data").decode().splitlines()
+        for name in artifacts:
+            self.assertEqual(git("hash-object", name).strip(), git("rev-parse", BASE + ":" + name).strip(), name)
+        print("P10 original_test_bodies=%d unchanged_git_artifacts=%d" % (count, len(artifacts)), flush=True)
+
+    def test_p11_default_msa_and_spc_report_bytes_and_repeat(self):

195:         self.limits = limits
196:         self.allowance = tuning.cusum_k * limits.sigma_hat
197:         self.bound = tuning.cusum_h * limits.sigma_hat

201:     def update(self, x):
202:         step = x - self.limits.xbarbar
203:         self.plus.append(max(0.0, (self.plus[-1] if self.plus else 0.0) + step - self.allowance))

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes" / "lamp_models_retest.json"
STAGE_ONE = ROOT / "recipes" / "lamp_models.json"
EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment.json"
FIXTURES = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures"


def run_cli(directory, input_path, recipe_path=RECIPE, equipment_path=EQUIPMENT):
    command = ["py", "-m", "src.main", str(input_path)]


class EquipmentReportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_sample_t33_to_t39_reports_and_all_rejected_state_unchanged(self):
        sample = ROOT / "data" / "sample_events_equipment.csv"
        models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
        process = RecipeProcess(models, load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, models))
        with sample.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
            events = list(csv.DictReader(source))
        rejected = []
        for row in events:
            before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
            decision = process.apply(row)
            if not decision.accepted:
                self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
                rejected.append(decision.rule_id)
        self.assertEqual(len(events), 94)
        self.assertEqual(rejected, ["RULE_EQUIP", "RULE_EQUIP", "RULE_EQUIP", "RULE_EQUIP",
                                    "RULE_PRESERVE", "RULE_PRESERVE", "RULE_INSP_PRE"])
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T36"].status, "폐기")
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T33_T35_A"].retest_counts, {"LEAK": 1})
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T33_T35_A"].status, "공정 진행 중(P3 완료)")
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T34"].inspections, {"LEAK": "PASS"})
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T38"].inspections, {})
        self.assertEqual(process.products["T39_EARLY"].inspections, {})
        self.assertEqual((process.equipment["LEAK-1"].counted_results, process.equipment["LEAK-1"].fail_count), (12, 5))
        self.assertEqual((process.equipment["LEAK-2"].counted_results, process.equipment["LEAK-2"].fail_count), (3, 2))
        self.assertEqual([state.state for state in process.equipment.values()], ["정지", "정지", "가동", "가동"])
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            result = run_cli(directory, sample)
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
            for suffix in ("rows", "products", "rejections", "equipment"):
                name = "sample_events_equipment_" + suffix + ".csv"
                self.assertEqual((Path(directory) / "reports" / name).read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / name).read_bytes())

    def write_input(self, directory):
        events = []

        def add(product_id, event, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
            events.append((str(len(events) + 1), product_id, event, inspection, result, model, equipment))

+"""Review 021: focused probes; all generated outputs use tempfile directories."""
+
+import ast
+import csv
+from decimal import Decimal, localcontext
+from fractions import Fraction
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+from src import anomaly, msa, spc
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "5d46efe9975be42e18fb5b99ceda726e55984c88"
+UNIT = spc.Limits(0.0, 2.326, 1.0, 3.0, -3.0, 10.0, 1.0, 2)
+TUNING = spc.Tuning(0.2, 3, 0.5, 1.5)
+
+
+def raw(name):
+    return json.loads((ROOT / "recipes" / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+
+
+def dump(path, data):
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def cli(module, *args, cwd):
+    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT), PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8", PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=cwd, env=env,
+                          capture_output=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=240)
+
+
+def rows(path):
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def write_rows(path, records):
+    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=tuple(records[0]))
+        writer.writeheader()
+        writer.writerows(records)
+
+
+def generator(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("generator_measured.json")
+    data.update(recipe=str(ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_measured.json"),
+                equipment=str(ROOT / "recipes/equipment_measured.json"))
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "generator.json", data)
+
+
+def msa_config(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("msa_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), parts=2, operators=2, trials=2,
+                products=30, dev_seeds="0-0", holdout_seeds="100-100", gauges={"good": data["gauges"]["good"]})
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "msa.json", data)
+
+
+def prepare(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("anomaly_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), length=40, window=4,
+                onset_min=12, onset_max=25, iterations=2,
+                splits={name: {"seed_start": start, "count": 12}
+                        for name, start in zip(anomaly.SPLITS, (0, 100, 200))})
+    data.update(changes)
+    anomaly_path = dump(Path(folder) / "anomaly.json", data)
+    result = cli("src.anomaly", anomaly_path, cwd=folder)
+    if result.returncode:
+        raise AssertionError(result.stderr)
+    config = raw("spc_lamp.json")
+    config.update(anomaly="anomaly.json", anomaly_report="reports/anomaly_methods.csv",
+                  anomaly_model="reports/anomaly_model.csv")
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "spc.json", config)
+
+
+class Probe021(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_p01_exact_guards_at_negative_bounds_and_tiny_width(self):
+        tiny = "0." + "0" * 34 + "1"
+        for lower, upper, expected in (("7", "13", ("7.001", "12.999")),
+                                       ("-13", "-7", ("-12.999", "-7.001")),
+                                       ("-3", "3", ("-2.999", "2.999"))):
+            self.assertEqual(tuple(map(str, msa.acceptance_limits(lower, upper, tiny, 0.1))), expected)
+        self.assertIsNone(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "1" + "0" * 40, 0.1))
+        self.assertEqual(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "0", 0.1), (Decimal("7"), Decimal("13")))
+        print("P01 tiny/negative/huge/zero guards: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p02_fixed_msa_extremes_complete_cli(self):
+        for case in ("large_guard", "large_cpk"):
+            with self.subTest(case=case), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                changes = ({"guard_multipliers": ["1" + "0" * 40]} if case == "large_guard"
+                           else {"generator": str(generator(folder, process_cpk=1e200))})
+                result = cli("src.msa", msa_config(folder, **changes), cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                reports = Path(folder) / "reports"
+                self.assertEqual(len(list(reports.glob("msa_*.csv"))), 3)
+                if case == "large_guard":
+                    dev = next(row for row in rows(reports / "msa_guard.csv") if row["section"] == "development")
+                    self.assertEqual(dev["status"], msa.NO_GUARD)
+                else:
+                    self.assertEqual(Decimal(rows(reports / "msa_grr.csv")[0]["cpk_obs"]), Decimal("1e200"))
+                print("P02 %s: exit=0, reports=3" % case, flush=True)
+
+    def test_p03_observed_cpk_across_scales_and_display_ties(self):
+        for sigma in (1e-200, 1.0, 1e100):
+            cpk = 1 / sigma
+            for ratio in (0, 0.5, 2):
+                value = msa.observed_cpk(cpk, sigma, ratio * sigma)
+                self.assertAlmostEqual(value / cpk, 1 / math.hypot(1, ratio))
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(1.23445), "1.2345")
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(-1.23445), "-1.2345")
+        print("P03 scale and signed HALF_UP: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p04_tiny_valid_ewma_lambda_keeps_positive_limit(self):
+        tuning = spc.Tuning(1e-20, 3, 0.5, 1.5)
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = raw("spc_lamp.json")
+            config["ewma_lambda"] = tuning.ewma_lambda
+            loaded = spc.load_settings(dump(Path(folder) / "spc.json", config))
+            self.assertEqual(loaded.tuning.ewma_lambda, tuning.ewma_lambda)
+        with localcontext() as context:
+            context.prec = 90
+            weight = Decimal("1e-20")
+            z = Decimal(0)
+            for t in range(12):
+                z = weight * Decimal("0.1") + (1 - weight) * z
+                limit = 3 * (weight / (2 - weight) * (1 - (1 - weight) ** (2 * (t + 1)))).sqrt()
+                self.assertLess(z, limit)  # Independent high-precision oracle: no crossing at any t.
+        chart = spc.Ewma(UNIT, tuning)
+        actual = spc.first_alarm(chart, [0.1] * 12, 9)
+        stable_limit = 3 * math.sqrt(tuning.ewma_lambda / (2 - tuning.ewma_lambda)
+                                     * -math.expm1(2 * (chart.index + 1) * math.log1p(-tuning.ewma_lambda)))
+        print("P04 alarm=%r limit=%r expected_no_alarm stable_limit=%r" %
+              (actual, chart.limit, stable_limit), flush=True)
+        self.assertIsNone(actual, "Valid lambda collapses the EWMA limit to zero")
+
+    def test_p05_prewarm_and_joint_rules_at_offgrid_start(self):
+        limits = spc.Limits(0, 2.326, 1, 10, -10, 1, 1, 8)
+        values = [2.5] * 10 + [0.5, 1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 4.5]
+        alarm, active = spc.subgroup_alarm(values, limits, 10, True)
+        self.assertEqual((alarm, active), (14, ["range", "run_2of3"]))
+        counts = spc.count_triggers([active])
+        self.assertEqual((counts["range"], counts["run_2of3"], counts["joint"]), (1, 0, 1))
+        self.assertEqual(spc.first_alarm(spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING), [0, 0, -1, -2], 0), 3)
+        chart = spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING)
+        for value in (0, 0, -1, -2):
+            chart.update(value)
+        self.assertEqual(chart.change_point(), 2)
+        print("P05 prewarm, range/run priority and lower CUSUM: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p06_decision_denominator_exhaustive_offgrid_intervals(self):
+        for first in range(2, 15):
+            for last in range(0, 31):
+                expected = sum(t % 5 == 4 for t in range(first, last + 1))
+                self.assertEqual(spc.decision_count("xbar_we", first, last), expected)
+        outcomes = [(0, None, 14), (1, 13, None), (1, 18, 14)]
+        self.assertEqual(spc.false_per_1000_decisions("xbar_r", outcomes, 40, 10), Fraction(2000, 2))
+        print("P06 403 offgrid intervals and pre-onset exposure: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p07_conflicting_duplicate_reference_rows_must_not_be_hidden(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = prepare(folder)
+            path = Path(folder) / "reports/anomaly_methods.csv"
+            records = rows(path)
+            original = next(row for row in records if row["section"] == "validation" and row["method"] == "rule_3_2")
+            wrong = dict(original, mean_delay_cost="999.0000")
+            records.insert(0, wrong)  # Last-wins dict currently hides the conflicting row.
+            write_rows(path, records)
+            first = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            write_rows(path, records[1:] + [wrong])
+            second = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            actual = (first.returncode, second.returncode)
+            print("P07 conflicting reference duplicate order exits=%r" % (actual,), flush=True)
+            self.assertEqual(actual, (2, 2), first.stderr + second.stderr)
+
+    def test_p08_rule_metrics_collision_does_not_prove_same_measurements(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            gen = generator(folder, process_cpk=5, defective_product_probability=0)
+            config = prepare(folder, generator=str(gen))
+            settings = anomaly.load_settings(Path(folder) / "anomaly.json")
+            before_process = anomaly.load_process(settings)
+            before = anomaly.build_split(settings.splits["validation"], settings, before_process)
+            generator(folder, process_cpk=6, defective_product_probability=0)
+            after_process = anomaly.load_process(settings)
+            after = anomaly.build_split(settings.splits["validation"], settings, after_process)
+            self.assertNotEqual([s.values for s in before], [s.values for s in after])
+            self.assertEqual(anomaly.rule_outcomes(before, settings), anomaly.rule_outcomes(after, settings))
+            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            print("P08 different_measurements=True identical_rule_outcomes=True exit=%d" % result.returncode,
+                  flush=True)
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_p09_invalid_config_then_valid_input(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = prepare(folder)
+            good = json.loads(config.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+            for bad in ([], dict(good, ewma_lambda=True), dict(good, cusum_h=float("nan")),
+                        {key: value for key, value in good.items() if key != "anomaly_model"}):
+                dump(config, bad)
+                result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 3, result.stderr)
+                self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports/spc_methods.csv").exists())
+            dump(config, good)
+            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            print("P09 structural/bool/NaN/missing refusal followed by valid run: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p10_original_tests_and_artifacts_preserved(self):
+        def git(*args):
+            return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
+
+        def bodies(text):
+            found = {}
+            def visit(node, prefix=()):
+                for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
+                    if isinstance(child, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
+                        key = prefix + (child.name,)
+                        if child.name.startswith("test_"):
+                            found[key] = ast.dump(child, include_attributes=False)
+                        visit(child, key)
+                    else:
+                        visit(child, prefix)
+            visit(ast.parse(text))
+            return found
+
+        count = 0
+        for name in git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "tests").decode().splitlines():
+            if name.endswith(".py"):
+                before = bodies(git("show", BASE + ":" + name).decode("utf-8-sig"))
+                after = bodies((ROOT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+                for key, value in before.items():
+                    self.assertEqual(after.get(key), value, name + ":" + ".".join(key))
+                    count += 1
+        artifacts = git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "reports", "recipes", "data").decode().splitlines()
+        for name in artifacts:
+            self.assertEqual(git("hash-object", name).strip(), git("rev-parse", BASE + ":" + name).strip(), name)
+        print("P10 original_test_bodies=%d unchanged_git_artifacts=%d" % (count, len(artifacts)), flush=True)
+
+    def test_p11_default_msa_and_spc_report_bytes_and_repeat(self):
+        reference_before = {path: path.read_bytes() for path in (ROOT / "reports").glob("anomaly_lamp_*.csv")}
+        for module, config_name in (("src.msa", "msa_lamp.json"), ("src.spc", "spc_lamp.json")):
+            with self.subTest(module=module), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                result = cli(module, ROOT / "recipes" / config_name, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                generated = sorted((Path(folder) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+                self.assertEqual(len(generated), 3 if module == "src.msa" else 2)
+                for path in generated:
+                    self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+                print("P11 %s stored_bytes_equal=%d" % (module, len(generated)), flush=True)
+                if module == "src.spc":
+                    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as second:
+                        repeated = cli(module, ROOT / "recipes" / config_name, cwd=second)
+                        self.assertEqual(repeated.returncode, 0, repeated.stderr)
+                        for path in generated:
+                            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (Path(second) / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes())
+        for path, content in reference_before.items():
+            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), content, str(path))
+        print("P11 anomaly input reports raw bytes unchanged; SPC repeat identical", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p12_legacy_sample_reports_regenerate_unchanged(self):
+        cases = (("sample_events", ()), ("sample_events_v2", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models.json")),
+                 ("sample_events_retest", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json")),
+                 ("sample_events_equipment", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json", "--equipment",
+                                              ROOT / "recipes/equipment.json")))
+        for stem, args in cases:
+            with self.subTest(stem=stem), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                result = cli("src.main", ROOT / "data" / (stem + ".csv"), *args, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                generated = list((Path(folder) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+                self.assertEqual(len(generated), 4 if stem.endswith("equipment") else 3)
+                for path in generated:
+                    self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+                print("P12 %s raw_bytes_equal=%d" % (stem, len(generated)), flush=True)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+| 이전 항목 | 심각도 | 재판정 | 근거 |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| R20-H1 | 높음 | 해결 | `src/msa.py:412–415`가 Fraction과 정수 ceil/floor로 전체 한계를 계산한다. P01은 미세 양수 `10^-35`, 음수 규격, 0 배수, `10^40` 배수를 확인했다. 미세 폭은 7.001/12.999이고 큰 폭은 None이다. P02의 큰 배수 CLI도 종료 0·보고서 3개·가드 불가를 확인했다. |
+| R20-H2 | 높음 | 해결 | `src/msa.py:479`가 Decimal quantize 대신 정수 기반 `line.show`를 사용한다. P02의 유효 Cpk=`1e200` 전체 CLI가 종료 0·보고서 3개로 끝나고 관측 Cpk도 `1e200`이다. P03의 양·음 반올림 동점도 HALF_UP과 맞는다. |
+| R20-H3 | 높음 | 해결 | `src/msa.py:350`이 `cpk / hypot(1, GRR/σ)`를 사용한다. P03의 σ=`1e-200`, GRR=0 및 양수 비율에서 제곱 언더플로 없이 식과 일치했다. P02에서 H2 수정 뒤 전체 보고 경로까지 완주했다. |
+
+gpt-log의 세 수정 설명은 위 새 반례와 코드가 뒷받침하므로 수용한다. 세 항목에 대한 별도 반박은 없었다. 이전 반례 파일 전체를 다시 실행하지 않았다.
+
+T215에 대한 Claude의 명세 이견도 수용한다. `C⁺_0 = 2 − 0.5 = 1.5`는 `hσ̂=1.5`를 **초과하지 않는다**. 수식대로 첫 초과는 1이고 판단 시작 뒤 첫 경보는 9다. 사전 기대표는 수정하지 않았다. 낮음 기록: `handoff/tasks.md:140`의 “첫 초과 0”은 수식과 모순되는 설명문이다. 핵심 기대인 경보 9는 유지되므로 별도의 중간 이상 수정 요구로 세지 않는다. 제안은 이 명세 이견을 결정 기록에서 명확히 하는 것이다. 기존 “마지막 거절 쌍” 문구의 낮음도 새 수정 요구로 다시 세지 않는다.
+
+**보존 및 현행 요구 확인**
+
+- P10에서 기준 커밋의 기존 `test_*` 본문 **397개**가 클래스 경로를 포함한 AST 기준으로 동일했다. 기존 테스트 삭제·본문 변경은 없고 `tests/test_msa.py`에는 020 수정 회귀 3개만 추가됐다. 본문 보존은 확인했지만 전체 테스트 실행 통과와 구분한다.
+- P10에서 기존 설정·데이터·보고서 **113개**의 Git 정규화 내용 해시가 모두 같았다. 그중 기존 보고서는 84개다. 저장소의 LF/CRLF 차이를 정규화한 검사이므로 이것만으로 재생성 바이트 일치를 주장하지 않는다.
+- P11·P12에서 tempfile 아래 실제 CLI로 **기존 보고서 16개**(기본 MSA 3개, v1·v2·재검사·설비 샘플 13개)를 다시 생성하여 체크아웃된 저장본과 원시 바이트까지 같음을 확인했다. 이번에 재생성하지 않은 기존 보고서는 Git 내용 보존만 확인했다.
+- P11에서 새 기본 SPC 보고서 2개도 저장본과 바이트 동일했고, 별도 두 번째 임시 폴더 실행과도 바이트 동일했다. SPC가 읽은 기존 `anomaly_lamp_*` 보고서 3개는 실행 전후 원시 바이트가 동일했다. 전체 11단계 학습을 다시 수행하지는 않았다.
+- `6a4a2c9..HEAD`의 13단계 구현은 기존 코드·테스트·설정·데이터·보고서를 수정하지 않고 SPC 파일을 추가했다. 지정 전체 diff에 있는 기존 msa 코드·테스트 수정은 그 앞의 별도 020 수정 커밋이다. 필요한 재검토 수정과 13단계의 보존 위반을 혼동하지 않았다.
+- 부분군 추정은 학습 정상 시나리오만 쓰고 잔여 값을 버린다. 런 규칙은 첫 부분군부터 누적하되 판단 시점 이후에만 인정한다. P05의 비정렬 시작 시점·범위/런 동시 경보·아래쪽 CUSUM과 P06의 403개 구간 판단 수가 식과 일치했다.
+- 선택은 validation의 Metrics만 사용하고 holdout으로 다시 고르지 않는다. `clean_holdout`과 참조 행도 후보에 넣지 않는다. 기본 결과의 `기준 충족 관리도 없음` 및 README 표는 재생성 보고서와 일치했다. gpt-log의 기준 미달 결과 유지 설명을 수용한다.
+- `load_reference`·`check_model`의 설정 불일치·누락 검사는 존재하지만, P07·P08의 문제가 남는다. 나머지 T205–T220·T251–T257의 원본 테스트 실행 통과는 이번 환경에서 미검증이다. 구현자 로그의 447개 OK를 이번 직접 실행 결과로 대신하지 않았다.
+
+**이번 반례**
+
+파일: `handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_021.py`. 총 12개 메서드 중 **9개 통과·3개 실패**다. P04·P07은 첫 실행 뒤 검증 근거를 보강하여 두 메서드만 다시 실행했고 동일한 제품 문제를 확인했다. 아래 실제 열은 보강 실행까지 반영했다.

+
+P01의 음수 규격, P03의 스케일 조합, P04·P05·P06·P07·P08·P09 등 최소 5개 이상이 명세·기대표 밖의 입력·규칙 조합이다. 생성 파일은 모두 tempfile 안에 두었고 저장소 reports/data를 덮어쓰지 않았다.
+
+**실행 명령과 결과**
+
+전체 테스트는 아래 명령을 **한 번만 직접 호출**했다. 런처가 실패하여 unittest는 시작하지 못했다. 전체 테스트 재시도·우회 실행은 하지 않았다.
+
+~~~powershell
+py -m unittest discover -s tests
+~~~
+
+종료 코드 1, 출력 원문:
+
+~~~text
+No installed Python found!
+~~~
+
+지정 반례 명령도 직접 호출했다. 반례 실행에는 `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`을 적용했다.
+
+~~~powershell
+py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_021.py
+~~~
+
+종료 코드 1, 출력 원문:
+
+~~~text
+No installed Python found!
+~~~
+
+설치된 Python 3.12 인터프리터로 **이번 반례만** 실행했다. 환경·런처·원본 테스트·패키지는 바꾸지 않았다. `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`을 적용했고 자식 CLI도 `-B`로 실행했다.
+
+~~~powershell
+& 'C:\Users\PC\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python312\python.exe' -B -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_021.py
+~~~

+"""Review 021: focused probes; all generated outputs use tempfile directories."""
+
+import ast
+import csv
+from decimal import Decimal, localcontext
+from fractions import Fraction
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+from src import anomaly, msa, spc
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "5d46efe9975be42e18fb5b99ceda726e55984c88"
+UNIT = spc.Limits(0.0, 2.326, 1.0, 3.0, -3.0, 10.0, 1.0, 2)
+TUNING = spc.Tuning(0.2, 3, 0.5, 1.5)
+
+
+def raw(name):
+    return json.loads((ROOT / "recipes" / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+
+
+def dump(path, data):
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def cli(module, *args, cwd):
+    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT), PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8", PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=cwd, env=env,
+                          capture_output=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=240)
+
+
+def rows(path):
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def write_rows(path, records):
+    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=tuple(records[0]))
+        writer.writeheader()
+        writer.writerows(records)
+
+
+def generator(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("generator_measured.json")
+    data.update(recipe=str(ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_measured.json"),
+                equipment=str(ROOT / "recipes/equipment_measured.json"))
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "generator.json", data)
+
+
+def msa_config(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("msa_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), parts=2, operators=2, trials=2,
+                products=30, dev_seeds="0-0", holdout_seeds="100-100", gauges={"good": data["gauges"]["good"]})
+    data.update(changes)
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "msa.json", data)
+
+
+def prepare(folder, **changes):
+    data = raw("anomaly_lamp.json")
+    data.update(generator=str(ROOT / "recipes/generator_measured.json"), length=40, window=4,
+                onset_min=12, onset_max=25, iterations=2,
+                splits={name: {"seed_start": start, "count": 12}
+                        for name, start in zip(anomaly.SPLITS, (0, 100, 200))})
+    data.update(changes)
+    anomaly_path = dump(Path(folder) / "anomaly.json", data)
+    result = cli("src.anomaly", anomaly_path, cwd=folder)
+    if result.returncode:
+        raise AssertionError(result.stderr)
+    config = raw("spc_lamp.json")
+    config.update(anomaly="anomaly.json", anomaly_report="reports/anomaly_methods.csv",
+                  anomaly_model="reports/anomaly_model.csv")
+    return dump(Path(folder) / "spc.json", config)
+
+
+class Probe021(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_p01_exact_guards_at_negative_bounds_and_tiny_width(self):
+        tiny = "0." + "0" * 34 + "1"
+        for lower, upper, expected in (("7", "13", ("7.001", "12.999")),
+                                       ("-13", "-7", ("-12.999", "-7.001")),
+                                       ("-3", "3", ("-2.999", "2.999"))):
+            self.assertEqual(tuple(map(str, msa.acceptance_limits(lower, upper, tiny, 0.1))), expected)
+        self.assertIsNone(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "1" + "0" * 40, 0.1))
+        self.assertEqual(msa.acceptance_limits("7", "13", "0", 0.1), (Decimal("7"), Decimal("13")))
+        print("P01 tiny/negative/huge/zero guards: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p02_fixed_msa_extremes_complete_cli(self):
+        for case in ("large_guard", "large_cpk"):
+            with self.subTest(case=case), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                changes = ({"guard_multipliers": ["1" + "0" * 40]} if case == "large_guard"
+                           else {"generator": str(generator(folder, process_cpk=1e200))})
+                result = cli("src.msa", msa_config(folder, **changes), cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                reports = Path(folder) / "reports"
+                self.assertEqual(len(list(reports.glob("msa_*.csv"))), 3)
+                if case == "large_guard":
+                    dev = next(row for row in rows(reports / "msa_guard.csv") if row["section"] == "development")
+                    self.assertEqual(dev["status"], msa.NO_GUARD)
+                else:
+                    self.assertEqual(Decimal(rows(reports / "msa_grr.csv")[0]["cpk_obs"]), Decimal("1e200"))
+                print("P02 %s: exit=0, reports=3" % case, flush=True)
+
+    def test_p03_observed_cpk_across_scales_and_display_ties(self):
+        for sigma in (1e-200, 1.0, 1e100):
+            cpk = 1 / sigma
+            for ratio in (0, 0.5, 2):
+                value = msa.observed_cpk(cpk, sigma, ratio * sigma)
+                self.assertAlmostEqual(value / cpk, 1 / math.hypot(1, ratio))
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(1.23445), "1.2345")
+        self.assertEqual(msa._text(-1.23445), "-1.2345")
+        print("P03 scale and signed HALF_UP: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p04_tiny_valid_ewma_lambda_keeps_positive_limit(self):
+        tuning = spc.Tuning(1e-20, 3, 0.5, 1.5)
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = raw("spc_lamp.json")
+            config["ewma_lambda"] = tuning.ewma_lambda
+            loaded = spc.load_settings(dump(Path(folder) / "spc.json", config))
+            self.assertEqual(loaded.tuning.ewma_lambda, tuning.ewma_lambda)
+        with localcontext() as context:
+            context.prec = 90
+            weight = Decimal("1e-20")
+            z = Decimal(0)
+            for t in range(12):
+                z = weight * Decimal("0.1") + (1 - weight) * z
+                limit = 3 * (weight / (2 - weight) * (1 - (1 - weight) ** (2 * (t + 1)))).sqrt()
+                self.assertLess(z, limit)  # Independent high-precision oracle: no crossing at any t.
+        chart = spc.Ewma(UNIT, tuning)
+        actual = spc.first_alarm(chart, [0.1] * 12, 9)
+        stable_limit = 3 * math.sqrt(tuning.ewma_lambda / (2 - tuning.ewma_lambda)
+                                     * -math.expm1(2 * (chart.index + 1) * math.log1p(-tuning.ewma_lambda)))
+        print("P04 alarm=%r limit=%r expected_no_alarm stable_limit=%r" %
+              (actual, chart.limit, stable_limit), flush=True)
+        self.assertIsNone(actual, "Valid lambda collapses the EWMA limit to zero")
+
+    def test_p05_prewarm_and_joint_rules_at_offgrid_start(self):
+        limits = spc.Limits(0, 2.326, 1, 10, -10, 1, 1, 8)
+        values = [2.5] * 10 + [0.5, 1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 4.5]
+        alarm, active = spc.subgroup_alarm(values, limits, 10, True)
+        self.assertEqual((alarm, active), (14, ["range", "run_2of3"]))
+        counts = spc.count_triggers([active])
+        self.assertEqual((counts["range"], counts["run_2of3"], counts["joint"]), (1, 0, 1))
+        self.assertEqual(spc.first_alarm(spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING), [0, 0, -1, -2], 0), 3)
+        chart = spc.Cusum(UNIT, TUNING)
+        for value in (0, 0, -1, -2):
+            chart.update(value)
+        self.assertEqual(chart.change_point(), 2)
+        print("P05 prewarm, range/run priority and lower CUSUM: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p06_decision_denominator_exhaustive_offgrid_intervals(self):
+        for first in range(2, 15):
+            for last in range(0, 31):
+                expected = sum(t % 5 == 4 for t in range(first, last + 1))
+                self.assertEqual(spc.decision_count("xbar_we", first, last), expected)
+        outcomes = [(0, None, 14), (1, 13, None), (1, 18, 14)]
+        self.assertEqual(spc.false_per_1000_decisions("xbar_r", outcomes, 40, 10), Fraction(2000, 2))
+        print("P06 403 offgrid intervals and pre-onset exposure: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p07_conflicting_duplicate_reference_rows_must_not_be_hidden(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = prepare(folder)
+            path = Path(folder) / "reports/anomaly_methods.csv"
+            records = rows(path)
+            original = next(row for row in records if row["section"] == "validation" and row["method"] == "rule_3_2")
+            wrong = dict(original, mean_delay_cost="999.0000")
+            records.insert(0, wrong)  # Last-wins dict currently hides the conflicting row.
+            write_rows(path, records)
+            first = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            write_rows(path, records[1:] + [wrong])
+            second = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            actual = (first.returncode, second.returncode)
+            print("P07 conflicting reference duplicate order exits=%r" % (actual,), flush=True)
+            self.assertEqual(actual, (2, 2), first.stderr + second.stderr)
+
+    def test_p08_rule_metrics_collision_does_not_prove_same_measurements(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            gen = generator(folder, process_cpk=5, defective_product_probability=0)
+            config = prepare(folder, generator=str(gen))
+            settings = anomaly.load_settings(Path(folder) / "anomaly.json")
+            before_process = anomaly.load_process(settings)
+            before = anomaly.build_split(settings.splits["validation"], settings, before_process)
+            generator(folder, process_cpk=6, defective_product_probability=0)
+            after_process = anomaly.load_process(settings)
+            after = anomaly.build_split(settings.splits["validation"], settings, after_process)
+            self.assertNotEqual([s.values for s in before], [s.values for s in after])
+            self.assertEqual(anomaly.rule_outcomes(before, settings), anomaly.rule_outcomes(after, settings))
+            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            print("P08 different_measurements=True identical_rule_outcomes=True exit=%d" % result.returncode,
+                  flush=True)
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_p09_invalid_config_then_valid_input(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            config = prepare(folder)
+            good = json.loads(config.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+            for bad in ([], dict(good, ewma_lambda=True), dict(good, cusum_h=float("nan")),
+                        {key: value for key, value in good.items() if key != "anomaly_model"}):
+                dump(config, bad)
+                result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 3, result.stderr)
+                self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports/spc_methods.csv").exists())
+            dump(config, good)
+            result = cli("src.spc", config, cwd=folder)
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            print("P09 structural/bool/NaN/missing refusal followed by valid run: correct", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p10_original_tests_and_artifacts_preserved(self):
+        def git(*args):
+            return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
+
+        def bodies(text):
+            found = {}
+            def visit(node, prefix=()):
+                for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
+                    if isinstance(child, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
+                        key = prefix + (child.name,)
+                        if child.name.startswith("test_"):
+                            found[key] = ast.dump(child, include_attributes=False)
+                        visit(child, key)
+                    else:
+                        visit(child, prefix)
+            visit(ast.parse(text))
+            return found
+
+        count = 0
+        for name in git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "tests").decode().splitlines():
+            if name.endswith(".py"):
+                before = bodies(git("show", BASE + ":" + name).decode("utf-8-sig"))
+                after = bodies((ROOT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+                for key, value in before.items():
+                    self.assertEqual(after.get(key), value, name + ":" + ".".join(key))
+                    count += 1
+        artifacts = git("ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE, "reports", "recipes", "data").decode().splitlines()
+        for name in artifacts:
+            self.assertEqual(git("hash-object", name).strip(), git("rev-parse", BASE + ":" + name).strip(), name)
+        print("P10 original_test_bodies=%d unchanged_git_artifacts=%d" % (count, len(artifacts)), flush=True)
+
+    def test_p11_default_msa_and_spc_report_bytes_and_repeat(self):
+        reference_before = {path: path.read_bytes() for path in (ROOT / "reports").glob("anomaly_lamp_*.csv")}
+        for module, config_name in (("src.msa", "msa_lamp.json"), ("src.spc", "spc_lamp.json")):
+            with self.subTest(module=module), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                result = cli(module, ROOT / "recipes" / config_name, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                generated = sorted((Path(folder) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+                self.assertEqual(len(generated), 3 if module == "src.msa" else 2)
+                for path in generated:
+                    self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+                print("P11 %s stored_bytes_equal=%d" % (module, len(generated)), flush=True)
+                if module == "src.spc":
+                    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as second:
+                        repeated = cli(module, ROOT / "recipes" / config_name, cwd=second)
+                        self.assertEqual(repeated.returncode, 0, repeated.stderr)
+                        for path in generated:
+                            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (Path(second) / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes())
+        for path, content in reference_before.items():
+            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), content, str(path))
+        print("P11 anomaly input reports raw bytes unchanged; SPC repeat identical", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p12_legacy_sample_reports_regenerate_unchanged(self):
+        cases = (("sample_events", ()), ("sample_events_v2", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models.json")),
+                 ("sample_events_retest", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json")),
+                 ("sample_events_equipment", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json", "--equipment",
+                                              ROOT / "recipes/equipment.json")))
+        for stem, args in cases:
+            with self.subTest(stem=stem), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+                result = cli("src.main", ROOT / "data" / (stem + ".csv"), *args, cwd=folder)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                generated = list((Path(folder) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+                self.assertEqual(len(generated), 4 if stem.endswith("equipment") else 3)
+                for path in generated:
+                    self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+                print("P12 %s raw_bytes_equal=%d" % (stem, len(generated)), flush=True)
+

# 리뷰 021 — 리뷰 020 재검토 및 v2 13단계 SPC 검수

검수일: 2026-10-31. 대상: `5d46efe9975be42e18fb5b99ceda726e55984c88..05329f2ba9e21e95f5e586ea5513ae7ccfbed33e`.

**판정 FIX. 리뷰 020의 높음 3건은 모두 해결됐다. 새 SPC 변경에서 높음 1건·중간 2건을 확인했다. 미해결 수정 요구는 3건이다.** 낮음은 수정 요구로 세지 않는다. 전체 기존 테스트 실행 통과는 런처 오류로 미검증이다.


전체 테스트 미검증은 실행 환경의 한계이며 제품 결함으로 계상하지 않았다. FIX는 독립 반례로 재현한 H1·M1·M2에 따른다. 이번 검수가 생성·수정한 파일은 이 리뷰와 `test_probe_021.py`뿐이다. 시작 때부터 존재한 `handoff/loop-logs/021-codex.txt`는 변경하지 않았다.
